```
- `status()` publishes a status as soon as it's subscribed to. After that, it publishes a new one at every whole minute from `ClockDataSource`, whenever the drink data source signals a change, and whenever the bedtime changes.
- The tips come from a Domain business rule that the repository executes, so no feature evaluates the decay function (VIEW-1 in <doc:CaffeineDecayModel>). The current level replaces VIEW-3's "found by date" lookup.
```
- `new_string`:

```
- `status(in:)` publishes a status as soon as it's subscribed to. After that, it publishes a new one at every whole minute from `ClockDataSource`, and whenever the drink data source signals a change (REPO-7 to REPO-10 in <doc:CaffeineDecayModel>). Nothing can change the bedtime yet, so it signals nothing. The data source that stores one will add a signal.
- The figure and the tips come from ``CaffeineStatusRule``, a Domain business rule that the repository executes, so no feature evaluates the decay function (VIEW-1 in <doc:CaffeineDecayModel>). It sums levels with ``CaffeineDecayRule``, so the figure, the tips, and the curve always agree. The current level replaces VIEW-3's "found by date" lookup.
```

```
- **The bedtime** is a ``Bedtime``: a time of day, not a date. Until onboarding (rank 6) or Settings (rank 21) stores one, it's 10:30pm, the prototype's example. The repository reads it from `BedtimeDataSource`, and `StandardBedtimeDataSource` always returns that default, following the half-life data source's pattern. The Last Cup card's cutoff will share it.
  - This design first read the bedtime from the profile data source. A dedicated data source keeps the profile to what the user has told the app, and matches how the half-life is read.
```

**2026-09-12 00:12:46 · Tool call: Edit**

- `replace_all`: `false`
- `file_path`: `~/Development/Femmli/Half-Life/Half-Life/Documentation.docc/TodayScreen.md`
- `old_string`: `` `CaffeineDecayFeature` observes the curve, the status, and the time of day. `ObserveTimeOfDayUseCase` supplies the time shown beside the hero figure and the position of the curve's "now" marker. ``
- `new_string`:

```
### CaffeineDecayFeature

`CaffeineDecayFeature` observes the curve, the status, and the time of day, and reduces each value into `State`. Its only command is `task`. `ObserveTimeOfDayUseCase` supplies the time shown beside the figure. The curve's "now" marker sits at the status's level, so the marker and the figure always agree.

`State.summary` chooses the card's sentence. It's presentation logic, so it lives in the feature and is tested there (DECAY-4):

| `summary` | When | Sentence |
|-----------|------|----------|
| `nil` | No status has arrived yet | None |
| `.clear` | No intake is counting | "Nothing in your system right now." |
| `.bedtimeAndHalfGone` | The last cup isn't half gone yet | "Down to about 34 mg by 11:00 PM — half of your last cup is gone by 4:27 PM." |
| `.bedtime` | The last cup is past half gone | "Down to about 34 mg by 11:00 PM." |

### The card

- **The figure** is the level now, rounded to whole milligrams, in `metricHero` light (80 pt at the default size, scaled with `@ScaledMetric(relativeTo: .largeTitle)`) and the accent color. Its "mg" is set at 45% of that size in `textSecondary`. The amount is a `Measurement` formatted with `usage: .asProvided`, so it stays in milligrams in every locale (constitution Article VII.3). The figure appears once the first status arrives.
- **The sentence** uses the same whole-milligram amounts, and clock times formatted for the locale.
- **The curve** shows the whole 24-hour window in Swift Charts: an area under a 2.5 pt `dataCaffeine` line, with a dashed rule and a dot at the current level. It's 160 pt high (`Sizing.curveHeight`), with no axes.
- **VoiceOver** reads the figure as one element: "In your system now", with the value "85 milligrams". The eyebrow and the time above it are hidden, because the figure says the same. The curve's label is "Caffeine over the day", and Swift Charts adds its own audio graph.
- **The AI chose, still to be confirmed:**
  - the wording "Nothing in your system right now." when nothing is counting
  - showing the whole window rather than, say, the next few hours
  - rounding every amount to whole milligrams, with "about" in the sentence rather than a range
```

- `new_string`:

```
| TODAY-1 | The greeting's actions reach `DailyGreetingFeature`, and its state changes appear under `TodayFeature.State.greeting`. |
| TODAY-2 | The decay card's actions reach `CaffeineDecayFeature`, and its state changes appear under `TodayFeature.State.caffeineDecay`. |

### Bedtime

| ID | Requirement |
|----|-------------|
| BED-1 | Until the user sets one, the bedtime is 10:30pm. |
| BED-2 | A bedtime is a real time of day, from 0:00 to 23:59. Anything else isn't created. |

### CaffeineStatusRule

Unit-tested directly, with the Caffeine Decay Model article's day of drinks. The rule is pure, so its tests need no fakes.

| ID | Requirement |
|----|-------------|
| STATUS-1 | The level is ``CaffeineDecayRule``'s level at the current time. At 3:00pm on the day of drinks, that's 295.44 mg. |
| STATUS-2 | The active intakes are the ones that count at the current time, in the order given. An intake consumed later, or one that's negligible now, isn't among them. |
| STATUS-3 | The last intake is half gone one half-life after it was consumed. Once that moment has passed, or when nothing is counting, there's no half-gone time. |
| STATUS-4 | The level at bedtime is ``CaffeineDecayRule``'s level at the next bedtime. |
| STATUS-5 | The next bedtime is the first at or after the current time: tonight's, exactly at bedtime, and tomorrow's after it. |
| STATUS-6 | The bedtime is a time of day in the given calendar's time zone. |

### Bedtime data source

| ID | Requirement |
|----|-------------|
| BEDSRC-1 | It returns the current bedtime. When no value has been stored, that's `Bedtime.standard` (10:30pm). |

### CaffeineDecayFeature

Tested with an exhaustive `TestStore`, with all three use cases overridden.

| ID | Requirement |
|----|-------------|
| DECAY-1 | `task` subscribes to the curve, and each curve is reduced into `State`. |
| DECAY-2 | `task` subscribes to the status, and each status is reduced into `State`. |
| DECAY-3 | `task` subscribes to the time of day, and each time of day is reduced into `State`. |
| DECAY-4 | `summary` is `nil` before the first status, `.clear` when nothing counts, both tips while the last cup is on its way to half gone, and only the level at bedtime after that. |
```

```
| DEP-1 | Live, `\.userProfileRepository` is a `LiveUserProfileRepository`, and `\.currentTimeRepository` is a `LiveCurrentTimeRepository`. |
```

- `new_string`:

```
| DEP-1 | `\.userProfileRepository` is a `LiveUserProfileRepository`, and `\.currentTimeRepository` is a `LiveCurrentTimeRepository`. Tests check this in the preview context, whose values are the live ones, because tests never read the live context. |
| DEP-2 | In a test, using a repository or use case that hasn't been overridden reports an issue. |
| DEP-3 | Each use case holds the one app-scoped repository that its repository key provides. |
| DEP-4 | `\.observeCaffeineStatus` holds the app-scoped `\.caffeineDecayRepository`. The preview repository opens an in-memory store, so this test runs inside the serialized `SwiftDataStoreTests`. |
```

```sh
echo "=== swift-format lint --strict"; xcrun swift-format lint --strict "${all[@]}"; echo "exit $?"
echo "=== swiftlint --strict"; swiftlint lint --strict --quiet "${all[@]}"; echo "exit $?"
echo "files checked: ${#all}"
```

```
    /// "IN YOUR SYSTEM NOW", with the time. VoiceOver reads both as one element, before the figure.
    private var header: some View {
        HStack(alignment: .firstTextBaseline) {
            Text("In your system now")
                .font(.eyebrow)
                .tracking(Typography.eyebrowTracking)
                .textCase(.uppercase)
            Spacer()
            if let timeOfDay = store.timeOfDay {
                Text(timeOfDay.date, format: Self.clockTime)
                    .font(.footnote)
                    .monospacedDigit()
            }
        }
        .foregroundStyle(Color.textSecondary)
        .accessibilityElement(children: .combine)
    }

    /// The hero figure: the number in the accent color, with a smaller unit beside it. VoiceOver reads the amount in
    /// full, such as "85 milligrams", because the heading above it says what it is.
    private func figure(_ milligrams: Double) -> some View {
        Text(heroText(milligrams))
            .accessibilityElement(children: .ignore)
            .accessibilityLabel(Text(Self.measurement(milligrams).formatted(Self.spokenAmount)))
            .accessibilityIdentifier(CaffeineDecayViewAccessibilityID.level)
    }
```

```sh
import XCTest

// Scratch only: prints the Today screen's element frames at the default and the largest text sizes.
final class LayoutDumpUITests: XCTestCase {
    @MainActor
    private func dump(_ marker: String, arguments: [String]) {
        let app = XCUIApplication()
        app.launchArguments += arguments
        app.launch()
        _ = app.descendants(matching: .any)[CaffeineDecayViewAccessibilityID.level].waitForExistence(timeout: 15)
        sleep(2)
        print("LAYOUT-\(marker)-START")
        print(app.debugDescription)
        print("LAYOUT-\(marker)-END")
        app.terminate()
    }

    @MainActor
    func testDumpFrames() {
        dump("DEFAULT", arguments: [])
        dump("LARGEST", arguments: ["-UIPreferredContentSizeCategoryName", "UICTContentSizeCategoryAccessibilityXXXL"])
    }
}
EOF

for m in DEFAULT LARGEST; do

  sed -n "/LAYOUT-$m-START/,/LAYOUT-$m-END/p" $S/layout-dump.log | grep -E "Window, |ScrollView|Other, .*(identifier: 'todayView|identifier: 'dailyGreeting)|IN YOUR SYSTEM NOW|caffeineDecayView|milligram|Caffeine over the day|Nothing in your system|GOOD (MORNING|AFTERNOON|EVENING)" | sed -E 's/^ +//; s/0x[0-9a-f]+, //' | cut -c1-200 | head -14
done
rm $W/Half-LifeUITests/LayoutDumpUITests.swift
```

**2026-09-12 00:30:35 · Tool call: Bash** — Test tracking and ViewThatFits variants of the card header

```sh
cd ~/Development/Femmli/Half-Life; S=/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-adamure-Development-Femmli-Half-Life/df99ce49-c9e4-4625-a61d-5028c4afc493/scratchpad; W=$S/wt; SIM=983286C9-749C-440C-BCEC-94A77E0E626B
V=Half-Life/Features/CaffeineDecay/CaffeineDecayView.swift
cat > $S/header-fits.py <<'EOF'
import re, sys
path, conditional = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2] == "conditional"
s = open(path).read()
block = re.search(r"    private var header: some View \{\n.*?\n    \}\n", s, re.S)
assert block, "header block not found"
tracking = ("dynamicTypeSize.isAccessibilitySize ? 0 : Typography.eyebrowTracking" if conditional
            else "Typography.eyebrowTracking")
new = f"""    private var header: some View {{
        ViewThatFits(in: .horizontal) {{
            HStack(alignment: .firstTextBaseline) {{
                heading
                Spacer()
                time
            }}
            VStack(alignment: .leading) {{
                heading
                time
            }}
        }}
        .foregroundStyle(Color.textSecondary)
        .accessibilityElement(children: .combine)
    }}

    private var heading: some View {{
        Text("In your system now")
            .font(.eyebrow)
            .tracking({tracking})
            .textCase(.uppercase)
            .fixedSize(horizontal: false, vertical: true)
    }}

    @ViewBuilder private var time: some View {{
        if let timeOfDay = store.timeOfDay {{
            Text(timeOfDay.date, format: Self.clockTime)
                .font(.footnote)
                .monospacedDigit()
        }}
    }}
"""
s = s.replace(block.group(0), new)
if conditional:
    anchor = "    @ScaledMetric(relativeTo: .largeTitle) private var heroSize = Typography.metricHeroSize\n"
    assert s.count(anchor) == 1, "scaled metric anchor not found"
    s = s.replace(anchor, anchor + "    @Environment(\\.dynamicTypeSize) private var dynamicTypeSize\n")
open(path, "w").write(s)
EOF
run_variant() {
  local name=$1 log=$S/header2-$1.log; shift
  cp $V $W/$V; "$@" || { echo "$name: patch failed"; return; }
  for attempt in 1 2; do
    xcrun simctl shutdown $SIM >/dev/null 2>&1; xcrun simctl erase $SIM; xcrun simctl boot $SIM; sleep 10
    (cd $W && xcodebuild test -project Half-Life.xcodeproj -scheme Half-Life -skipMacroValidation -destination "id=$SIM" -derivedDataPath $S/dd -clonedSourcePackagesDirPath $S/sp -only-testing:Half-LifeUITests/TodayUITests > $log 2>&1)
    grep -q 'failed preflight checks' $log || break
  done
  local result=$(grep -oE 'TEST (SUCCEEDED|FAILED)' $log | tail -1)
  local issues=$(grep -o 'AUDIT-ISSUE.*' $log | sed -E "s/.*compact=([^|]*) detail.*label: '([^']*)'.*/\1 on '\2'/" | sort -u | tr '\n' ';')
  local tests=$(grep -E "Test Case '-\[Half_LifeUITests\.TodayUITests" $log | grep -E 'passed|failed' | sed -E "s/.*TodayUITests ([A-Za-z]+)\]' (passed|failed).*/\2:\1/" | tr '\n' ' ')
  local other=$(grep -E 'failed preflight|error:.*\.swift' $log | grep -v '/sp/' | sed 's#.*/wt/##' | sort -u | head -2 | tr '\n' ';')
  echo "$name: ${result:-no result} audit=[${issues}] tests=[${tests}] other=[${other}]"
}
run_variant no-tracking /usr/bin/python3 -c "import sys; p=sys.argv[1]; s=open(p).read(); o='                .tracking(Typography.eyebrowTracking)\n'; assert s.count(o)==1, s.count(o); open(p,'w').write(s.replace(o,''))" "$W/$V"
run_variant fits-tracking-kept /usr/bin/python3 $S/header-fits.py "$W/$V" kept
run_variant fits-tracking-conditional /usr/bin/python3 $S/header-fits.py "$W/$V" conditional
cp $W/$V $S/header-fits-conditional.swift; cp $V $W/$V
```

```sh
echo "unit passed: $(grep -cE 'Test case .* passed' build/full-final.log)  UI passed: $(grep -cE "Test Case '-\[.*\]' passed" build/full-final.log)"
echo "=== coverage"; xcrun xccov view --report --only-targets build/TestResults-Final.xcresult 2>&1 | head -6
```

```
    - A second round of variants settled it. Removing the tracking passed. Stacking the time under the heading with `ViewThatFits` failed the Dynamic Type check on the time. Tracking only at non-accessibility sizes failed the Dynamic Type check on the heading.
    - The owner removed the eyebrow's tracking everywhere at 00:42, and the Design System article records the rule. In the final run, the Today screen's audit and the root screen's audit both pass.
```

### 2026-09-12 00:52:21 · Claude

I built the decay card, and it's on the Today screen under the greeting. The final full test run in the live tree passes: 438 unit tests and 17 UI tests, no failures, and no compiler warnings. That includes the Today screen's and the root screen's accessibility audits. `Half-Life.app` coverage is 95.06%. Formatting, lint and the doc build are clean. Nothing is committed.

**What it shows:**
- **Caffeine in your system now,** as a large figure rounded to whole milligrams. It updates every minute.
- **One sentence of tips:** "Down to about 34 mg by 10:30 PM — half of your last cup is gone by 4:27 PM." Once the last cup is half gone it gives only the bedtime part, and with nothing counting it says "Nothing in your system right now."
- **The decay curve,** 24 hours around now, with a marker at the current level.

**How it works:**
- `CaffeineDecayRepository` gained a second stream, the "status": the level now and the two tips. It recalculates every minute and whenever a drink is logged. The curve still never recalculates on a timer. The Caffeine Decay Model article records that exception as REPO-2 plus REPO-7 to REPO-10.
- The decay session handed its repository to me for this. Since then, half-life-35 has switched the maths to Bateman absorption, with the 13-minute absorption half-life you chose. The final test run included that change.

**Decisions and changes:**
- **Your call, 00:42:** you removed the +2 pt letter spacing from the small uppercase labels ("eyebrows") everywhere, including the greeting. With it, the card's heading failed the accessibility audit's "Text clipped" check once it wrapped at large text sizes. I found the cause by testing variants and measuring element frames.
- **My deviation from my own design:** the bedtime comes from its own data source, following the half-life's pattern, not from the profile. Until onboarding or Settings can store one, it's always 10:30 pm.
- **New design tokens:** an 80 pt hero figure that scales with Dynamic Type, a 45% size for the "mg" unit, and a 160 pt curve height.

**Things to know:**
- **Three red steps weren't seen on their own.** The build stopped at another file's error first. The log says which ones.
- **A bug in one of my new tests** made it depend on the simulator's time zone. It had never passed, and it now uses UTC.
- **Four greeting tests changed context:** they now check dependency wiring in the preview context instead of the live one. That follows a team rule that tests never use the live context. They check the same objects.
- **A layout problem remains:** at the largest text sizes, the time beside the card's heading squeezes into a narrow column. The audit doesn't flag it, and putting the time under the heading made the audit fail, so it needs another approach.

**Choices of mine to confirm** (they're also in the Today Screen article):
- the wording "Nothing in your system right now."
- showing the whole 24-hour curve rather than, say, the next few hours
- rounding every amount to whole milligrams, with "about" in the sentence
- after bedtime has passed, using tomorrow's bedtime for the tip

**Still not built:** Today total, Last cup, One tap, and History. They need their open design questions answered.

**Before you commit:** re-export the transcripts with `scripts/export_transcripts.py`. The two simulators I created, `HalfLife-Today-df99` and `HalfLife-Today-df99-b`, can be deleted with `xcrun simctl delete`.
